# CommandRetentionBudget/1

Pipeline source owner projection rule (2026-10-10): CPU slot6 retains the
artifact selected by the immutable original input: ordinary single-entry/module
artifact or OriginalDepthTableExpansionArtifact, never both. Graphics slots8/9
retain ordinary module child owners only; expanded entries belong to the retained
expansion artifact and have no fabricated separate child owners. The input's
explicit artifact profile determines projection before any native work. Empty
ordinary artifact fields in an expansion input must never be dereferenced.
Null validates ownership values, Vulkan/Metal retain their existing selected
payload refusals, and private WebGPU custody consumes the same projection without
gaining public authorization. Exact/one-less owner budgets remain mandatory.

Status: Contract Complete for L2 intent/lowering and fail-closed admission
prerequisites only. The L2 contract and common preparation
tools are prerequisites; no backend execution or platform claim follows from
them. This is independent of BindlessCross snapshot/pipeline/fault quotas.

L2 intent is an optional, explicitly opted-in command retention budget. The
descriptor names distinct retained resource owners, distinct query-pool owners,
timestamp write records, pipeline-statistics write records and CPU metadata
bytes. Owner counts bound backend-owned strong references, including resource,
staging and ordinary descriptor/argument storage. They do not count GPU bytes
or claim that one L2 resource always lowers to one native allocation. Per-backend
owner enumeration must follow actual registered ownership/binding relationships.
Query counts describe successful L2 write records; rejected/duplicate writes do
not consume a record. A zero count disables that category. Query writes require
a positive query-pool count. CPU metadata bytes must be positive and are an
independent hard ceiling, never inferred from snapshot bytes or object counts.

`CommandEncoderDesc::retentionBudget` is absent by default. Its presence requires
`optIn=true` and contractVersion=1. Absence requests no new retention contract
and does not certify bounded ordinary recording. Existing version-zero paths
retain their documented readiness/performance limitations. An unsupported
explicit request must fail before native command creation or staging serial
mutation; it may not proceed on the existing path or receive a placeholder.
The pure lowering plan supplies a stable diagnostic and factories report the
same diagnostic through RhiErrorHandler before returning null.

Preparation owns all metadata and its byte charge. Reuse core CpuMemoryQuotaScope
around actual core object/control-block/container allocations; allocator headers
retain the quota until each allocation is released. Zero is unlimited in that
existing core utility, so the RHI planner must reject zero before installing it.
Do not estimate real byte charge with sizeof(T)*requestedCount, or treat resource
count leases as CPU-byte evidence. Preparation allocation/quota failure returns
RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-PREPARATION-ALLOCATION-FAILED and no executable recorder.
Native allocations and GPU resource budgets retain their existing owners.

Recording must preflight the full batch of newly required owners and every
required query record before encoding or mutating resource/query state. Duplicate
owner pointer identity consumes no extra slot; adapters separately validate the
exact issuing registry/control block and logical resource identity. Batch failure
changes neither retained ownership nor query state. No allocation, waiting, IO,
shader compilation or pipeline creation is allowed in successful admission.
Existing core BoundedRetainer is the owner container; bounded query sequences
must reserve at preparation and enforce the declared count, not vector capacity.
Container element copying must itself be allocation-free (shared owner/value
records meet this constraint); the generic sequence cannot certify arbitrary T.

The serialized recording thread owns mutable containers. After complete native
submission preflight, move the prepared storage into completion-owned state with
no new allocation. Actual backend completion retires all resource/query owners
before publishing completion. Reserved metadata remains charged for as long as
its storage is allocated; clearing owners cannot fake a quota release. The
completion owner destroys storage when no further metadata observation is
required. Logical command
destruction cannot retire submitted ownership early. Unsubmitted abandonment
releases it deterministically. A recoverable preflight rejection may be retried
within the same existing budget; any rejection after native mutation poisons the
command and requires a new command/preparation, never rollback by guesswork.

Capability version, immutable lowering decision and execution must agree.
Native/Equivalent requires an implemented bounded recording payload and actual
completion retirement; a Validator result is not executable. Degraded or
DiagnosticFailure and version zero reject an explicit request. No device/OS/name
heuristic or automatic opt-in grants the contract. Stable diagnostics:

- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-VERSION
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-OPT-IN-REQUIRED
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-CPU-BUDGET
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-QUERY-POOL-BUDGET
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-UNSUPPORTED
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-PAYLOAD-MISSING
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-COMPLETION-MISSING
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-OWNER-CAPACITY
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-OWNER-MAPPING-CHANGED
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-QUERY-CAPACITY
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-PREPARATION-ALLOCATION-FAILED
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-CPU-QUOTA-MISMATCH
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-RECORDING-CLOSED
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-QUERY-OWNER-MISSING
- RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-QUERY-DUPLICATE

| Backend | Required lowering | Current conclusion |
|---|---|---|
| Null | Bounded Validator owner/record storage, logical liveness validation, NotExecuted completion; no GPU completion claim | DiagnosticFailure, version 0 until actual validator recording integration |
| Vulkan | Exact registered native resource/query owners, bounded recording, moved submission ownership, native completion retirement | DiagnosticFailure, version 0 until the payload and completion are proven |
| Metal | Exact registered resource/staging/argument/query owners, bounded recording, existing prepared submission owner, real native callback retirement | DiagnosticFailure, version 0 until all affected recording/query paths are proven |
| WebGPU | Equivalent bounded retained resource/query owners and scalar bindings, queue-completion proof and retirement; browser driver allocation excluded | DiagnosticFailure, version 0 until the payload and queue completion are proven |

This table changes only explicit CommandRetentionBudget/1 admission. Existing
validated BindlessCross subsets retain their own capability and execution gates.
No four-backend or whole-frame readiness claim is made by this prerequisite.

Acceptance requires quick pure contract/container tests, actual byte-quota
allocation/failure/lifetime evidence and Null hidden-frame. Backend Validated
additionally requires native resource/query execution, exact delayed completion
and cancellation, immutable budget gates before native mutation, and no core
allocations during successful admission/submit. Visual or rendering acceptance
requires AI RenderDoc capture; common ownership mechanics cannot substitute for
it. Platform Validated and Production Ready remain separate gates.

M4e5a evidence: twenty-four rebuilt CTests pass, including atomic batch
rejection/deduplication/move/clear without core allocation, declared sequence
count bounds and actual core byte-quota charge lifetime/failure. Null, Metal and
host WebGPU reject explicit requests; factories report the stable diagnostic
before native/staging mutation. Vulkan command/device-test syntax passes, but
no native Vulkan/MinGW acceptance is claimed. All four capability versions stay
zero. Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/retention-budget-report.yaml`,
`retention-budget-regression-results.xml` and `retention-budget-regression-tests.log`.

The next common storage increment composes resource/query owners with bounded
timestamp/statistics records. It requires an existing caller/backend-owned
CpuMemoryQuota controller whose byte limit exactly matches the request; that
controller's own allocation remains the provider's explicitly budgeted ownership.
The storage allocator must not create an unaccounted quota or substitute an
unlimited controller. Cross-category admission preflights all owner and record
requirements before retaining anything. Duplicate (pool owner,index,type) records
reject. Submission sealing freezes admission; completion retirement requires
backend completion proof, and unsubmitted abandonment is separate. None of these
ownership operations authorizes GPU execution or validates native query support.
Adapters use the separate, read-only preflight entry points before native
mutation and commit admission on the same serialized recording thread. An
external Boolean is only a transport for the adapter's completion proof; it
is not a synthetic completion primitive or evidence of GPU completion.

Metal query ownership transfer prerequisite: after every submission mark
succeeds, the existing preparation-owned submission container takes the exact
recording vectors by move, using their common core memory resource. The native
handler captures only an alias of that existing owner, never query vectors by
value. It resolves queries before releasing all pool and record references,
then publishes completion. Unsubmitted abandonment and terminal recording
failure release their recording-side query references. A failed precommit rolls
back query marks before retiring their references; submitted ownership cannot
be abandoned. This preserves existing query semantics without certifying the
still-unbounded recording path. Null/Vulkan/WebGPU payloads are unchanged and
all four explicit CommandRetentionBudget/1 conclusions remain DiagnosticFailure.
Host native counter probing requires the actual draw/dispatch sampling points;
stage-only support cannot prove this lowering or receive a fake query result.

M4e5b1 and M4e5b2a evidence: 27 rebuilt CTests pass under Metal API Validation,
including fast contracts, Null hidden-frame, bindless compiler/matrix contracts,
public Compute/images, private Graphics, delayed submit waits and recording
abandonment. Non-Apple Metal command/query stub and common-storage syntax pass.
The typed nonempty query metadata test preserves exact vector storage, rejects
foreign allocators/repeated transfers and retires CPU pool references with zero
core allocations. This is Contract Complete ownership evidence only. Actual
Apple M4 probes draw=0/dispatch=0/stage=1 and valid public timestamp/statistics
descriptors reject, so nonempty native query Backend Validated remains pending.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/query-transfer-report.yaml`,
`query-transfer-regression-results.xml` and `query-transfer-regression-tests.log`.

## Recording integration prerequisite: BufferCopy owners

This is an explicit backend-private prototype of CommandRetentionBudget/1,
accessible only with opted-in, already-prepared storage on the private native
factory. It does not change the public lowering table or grant L2 execution.
The first increment covered Transfer begin/end and BufferCopy; the subsequent
texture-transfer increment is described below. Remaining descriptor, pipeline, render, barrier,
query, indirect, debug, aliasing, secondary and indexed recording entries reject before mutation with
RHI-METAL-COMMAND-RETENTION-OPERATION-UNIMPLEMENTED. Indexed preparation counts
combined with this prerequisite reject before native creation. The full domain
remains the final target; this prerequisite must not be published as full support.

BufferCopy resolves both IDs through the exact backend registry, validates
native device, buffer kind, usages, destination transfer-write seal and byte
ranges, then preflights both registered
buffer-payload owners as a single batch. The buffer payload owns its actual native
buffer and allocation-release token; only those registered payload references
are retained. A budget failure encodes no copy and retains neither new owner.
Successful repeated copies deduplicate the same owners and allocate no core
metadata. No texture/sampler placeholder or fixed guessed owner budget is used.

The private factory charges actual command-local core metadata allocations
(capacity lease/control block, stats, command-buffer owner, native payload and
prepared submission owner) to the same supplied CPU quota as storage. The shared
backend queue/device and single capacity controller keep their existing separate
global ownership; their lazy initialization precedes the command quota scope.
Preparation failure
releases all partial charges and never returns an executable native payload.
The native blit-encoder owner is a separate actual allocation at pass begin;
recording BufferCopy does not allocate it per copy. Native driver and
Objective-C completed-handler block allocations
remain excluded from this core CPU metadata contract and must not be described
as bounded without independent evidence.

Finish seals storage; submit validates sealed ownership before mutation, moves
it into the existing prepared submission owner after all precommit gates, and
marks it submitted before commit. Actual native completion (including terminal
native error) is proof that encoded resource references can retire; it clears
owners and destroys the storage before completion publication. Unsubmitted
abandonment/poisoning and failed-precommit release ownership separately. A sealed
or submitted recorder rejects further recording even if its logical wrapper lives.
Null/Vulkan/WebGPU keep their explicit DiagnosticFailure/version-zero conclusions;
Metal public admission also remains DiagnosticFailure/version zero. Non-Apple
private factories reject this storage request with a stable runtime-unavailable
diagnostic rather than returning a diagnostic placeholder recorder.

M4e5b3a evidence: 28 rebuilt CTests pass, including actual opted-in private
BufferCopy with Metal API Validation, fast bounded/storage contracts and Null
hidden-frame. Thirty-two repeated copies allocate no core metadata; successful
submit adds zero core allocations without wait or one quota-charged native wait
owner with wait. Exact numerical data and owner retirement survive actual native
wait/completion with the logical command retained or destroyed. Zero/one/full
owner ceilings reject whole batches without changing destination data; sealed
destination and invalid resource reject before ownership changes. Command,
encoder and submit-wait metadata exhaustion, changed quota, unsubmitted
cancellation, terminal precommit failure and partial charge cleanup pass.
Status: Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for this private domain; scoped
Backend Validated for actual Metal copies and their ownership only. Public full
retention/Graphics, nonempty native queries, RenderDoc, platforms and Production
Ready remain separate unmet gates. Non-Apple Metal source/stub/test syntax passes.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/retention-copy-report.yaml`,
`retention-copy-regression-results.xml` and `retention-copy-regression-tests.log`.

## Texture-transfer recording integration prerequisite

The private prototype extends to BufferToTexture, TextureToBuffer,
TextureToTexture and existing staging buffer/texture copies only after their
exact ownership and data gates pass. Public versions stay zero on all four
backends; Null/Vulkan/WebGPU explicit budget requests remain DiagnosticFailure.
Metal ordinary texture validation/lowering preserves its existing format,
aspect, per-mip packing, ordered mip metadata, explicit extents, array-layer,
byte-range and managed synchronization rules. Shared schema row-pitch alignment
is the fact source. No format, offset, extent or owner count is inferred.

Use core PreflightReplay with a fresh deterministic mapper per pass to validate
every mip/layer before admission or native copy. Each mapper consumes immutable
caller metadata and retained registered/native objects on the serialized owner
thread. It uses only one stack plan at a time and no materialized plan array or
new region-count budget. Validation-only readback preflight admits no owners and
encodes nothing. A second-pass mapping failure is a terminal
RHI-METAL-TEXTURE-COPY-REPLAY-MAPPING-CHANGED diagnostic, poisons the command and
blocks finish/submit; no guessed rollback or partial success is permitted.

The registered resource's kind determines its exact owner. Buffer/sampler
entries have their one matching payload; TextureView-kind entries have exactly
one texture or texture-view payload. Missing, conflicting or mismatched payloads
reject. Texture views retain their native object and parent's allocation-release
token via the existing view contract; retaining an unrelated parent wrapper is
not a guessed substitute. Source and destination owner references are preflighted
as one batch, including the exact staging payload when used. Rejected layouts,
ranges or owner budgets retain nothing and encode no early mip. Existing staging
slice reservation/serial retirement remains separate from page-owner retention;
this increment does not certify pool reuse or all staging allocation behavior.

M4e5b3b evidence: 30 rebuilt CTests pass under Metal API Validation, including
quick contracts, Null hidden-frame, shader/matrix contracts, public Compute/
images, private Graphics, descriptor arrays, staging, ordinary upload/readback
and retained-allocation tests. Five transfer modes and four view modes record
32 times without core allocation; prepared transfer admission also succeeds
when actual CPU metadata quota is exhausted. Exact three-mip/two-layer texels,
untouched row padding, view-relative mip/layer mapping and delayed native
completion survive logical command retention/destruction. Late invalid metadata,
zero/one/full owner limits, validation-only, buffer seals and cancellation pass.
Views retain exact payload/allocation-token ownership through completion or
cancellation while their unrelated parent C++ payload can expire. Non-Apple
Metal source/stub/test syntax passes. The retained-allocation fixture now obtains
explicit immutable native descriptor snapshots rather than assuming logical
set/table metadata automatically owns native storage; dropping each snapshot
proves the actual heap charge lifetime. Status: Contract Complete / Prototype
Complete; scoped Backend Validated for private native transfers only. Four
public versions stay zero; no staging pool reuse, public Graphics, query,
RenderDoc/platform or Production Ready claim follows.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/retention-texture-report.yaml`,
`retention-texture-final-results.xml` and `retention-texture-final-tests.log`.

## Ordinary descriptor and pipeline integration prerequisite

DescriptorResourceLiveness/1 and DescriptorRecordedStorage/1 remain the fact
sources: public descriptor metadata names live IDs; an immutable native snapshot
or legal recorded operation owns the selected native storage. A descriptor write
alone is not a native-storage owner, GPU completion or permission to use a stale
ID. Existing prepared Compute/function/Graphics validators and actual native
binding-space reflection must be reused rather than duplicating a binding parser.

Ordinary array binding currently builds a temporary prepared-encoding vector,
creates real native argument buffers and grows a per-command cache at record
time. It cannot receive the retention contract merely by retaining two endpoint
owners or reusing a snapshot-count ceiling. Preparation must first produce an
immutable binding payload with its exact pipeline, selected resources and
argument buffers, declared count/CPU/GPU budgets and lifetime. Dynamic offsets
are explicit binding metadata; none may be guessed from a cached binding.

The next CPU prerequisite provides immutable indexed owner projection through
core BoundedRetainer and common CommandRetentionStorage. Validate every owner
and deduplicate against existing storage and the entire batch before mutation,
with no materialized temporary owner array. Projection callbacks must be
allocation-free, nonthrowing, deterministic and immutable on the serialized
owner thread; construction and mapping costs remain adapter-owned. Actual
recording must still validate issuing registry, liveness, kind, usage, native
device and compiled binding relationships. An unsupported/invalid descriptor
or missing prepared argument allocation fails before encoder/cache mutation.
Null/Vulkan/Metal/WebGPU public conclusions remain DiagnosticFailure/version0;
this helper changes no executable adapter, factory, pipeline or shader.

The projection API enforces an exact shared-owner result type and a nonthrowing
call operator at compile time. Owner mapping must remain immutable through
preflight and commit. Violating that precondition may expose partial CPU
retention; common storage returns RHI-COMMAND-RETENTION-OWNER-MAPPING-CHANGED,
and an adapter must terminally poison recording. This is not a recoverable
capacity failure or proof of native rollback. Public factories/execution gates
remain unchanged; no adapter currently consumes this new projection API.

M4e5b3c1 evidence: 3 focused tests and 30 rebuilt regressions pass. The generic
retainer reuses one atomic owner algorithm for pointer arrays and immutable
indexed projections. Missing late owners, deduplication, existing/exhausted
owner slots, empty input, source capacity after move, sealed common storage,
actual exhausted CPU quota, exact reference cleanup and reserved metadata
charge lifetime pass without core admission allocation. Non-Apple Metal/core
source/test/stub, Vulkan command/device-contract and WebGPU command syntax pass.
Existing native transfer/Compute/images/private Graphics/descriptor/staging/
wait/cancellation evidence remains intact. Status: Contract Complete for this
CPU prerequisite only; no native adapter uses it yet and no Backend Validated,
Platform Validated or Production Ready claim is made for the new API. Public
four-backend budget versions and public Metal Graphics stay zero.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/retention-projection-report.yaml`,
`retention-projection-regression-results.xml` and `retention-projection-regression-tests.log`.
The next ordinary binding increment must prepare exact scalar/array owner
relationships and argument storage before opening any recording gate.

## Ordinary prepared Compute binding prototype

Implementation order: the first native ordinary binding adapter covers direct
and array bindings with no dynamic offsets or push constants. The later uniform
dynamic-offset and full-block Compute push contracts below extend that private
subset. Other categories
must obtain explicit preparation/consumption contracts before their recording
gates open; rejection is diagnostic, never zero-offset substitution.
This is backend-private Prototype and does not replace the full ordinary domain
or close M4e5b3c2 until its remaining categories are proven. Null, Vulkan, Metal
and WebGPU public CommandRetentionBudget versions remain0 / DiagnosticFailure.

An explicit private preparation budget names contractVersion1, optIn, actual core
CPU metadata ceiling and native argument-buffer count/allocated-byte ceilings.
The supplied existing CPU quota must match exactly and may be the command's
quota; all snapshot/helper/argument-owner/control-block allocations made by
preparation are charged through core. Existing compiled pipeline/resources and
shared device/registry keep their previously declared budgets, separate from
preparation; no compilation or allocation occurs during successful binding.
Zero argument count/bytes allows direct-only preparation. Arrays require positive
counts/bytes sufficient for actual function encoders and queried allocatedSize.
Drivers/Objective-C blocks remain outside the core metadata guarantee.

Preparation takes the concrete issuing ComputePipeline and DescriptorSet, their
logical set slot and issuing registry; public layout/resource validators and
compiled native function mapping are authoritative. It creates immutable native
snapshot/function bindings using the existing function-level preparer. The
prepared ordinary wrapper is sealed, noncopyable and construction-key protected.
It observes descriptor lifetime/revision with core LifetimeObserver, owns an allocator-explicit
DescriptorConsumptionContract projection of actual L2 declared slots/reflected
bindings for completeness, and revalidates
exact registered resource payload/control-block identity, device, kind, parent
liveness, usage and write seals before new binding or dispatch. The native
pipeline is already compiled and provider-owned; no fabricated pipeline payload
or native-device-name authorization is introduced.

Command admission enumerates the whole immutable ownership graph: ordinary
prepared wrapper, function bindings, compiled pipeline, descriptor snapshot,
every selected native resource and every prepared argument buffer. Duplicate
exact payload identities consume no extra owner slot. Root/child ownership does
not bypass declared distinct-owner counting. Projection is allocation-free and
all owners must fit before pipeline/encoder/observation mutation. Capacity or
liveness failures preserve previous legal binding state. Mapper inconsistency
is terminal and poisons recording. All newly prepared storage uses the same
quota as command retention; a foreign or mutated quota cannot authorize binding.

Native Compute pass begin charges its existing encoder owner to the command
quota and poisons on metadata allocation failure. Ordinary binding records the
pipeline and prepared function bindings together after full validation/admission.
BoundDescriptorSlots provides bounded lifetime/revision observations and validates
all actual pipeline-required sets before direct dispatch. Pipeline switches reset
recording observations after successful admission. Pass end, abandonment and
poison clear recording-only aliases; common completion-owned storage remains the
sole in-flight ownership until actual native callback retirement/publication.
Descriptor rewrite/destruction or parent destruction prevents new consumption,
but legally recorded work keeps its immutable snapshots through completion.

Acceptance requires exact native A/B output for array/scalar sets, duplicate
rebind allocation0, missing required sets, late missing/foreign resources,
whole-owner count/CPU/native-argument budget failures, descriptor revisions,
arena/wrapper/parent liveness, immutable quota gates, cancellation and delayed
completion with logical commands retained/destroyed. Fast contracts and Null
hidden-frame remain mandatory. Numeric Compute evidence is not RenderDoc visual,
public Graphics, full retention, target-platform or Production Ready evidence.


M4e5b3c2a evidence: 32 rebuilt CTests pass with Metal API Validation. Both
scalar-only uniform/storage sets and sampled-texture-array + scalar multi-set
Compute produce exact A/B data. Rebind32 and dispatch add zero core allocations
at an exhausted actual quota. Flattened distinct owners are exactly11 scalar /
20 array for two immutable recordings. Actual argument allocatedSize budgets,
zero/one/one-less owner capacities, partial CPU preparation cleanup, hostile PMR
default, missing sets, quota/registry/pipeline failures, stale/revised/destroyed
bindings, parent destruction, seals, pipeline switches, cancellation and native
wait failure are covered. Real timeline-delayed completion retires exact native
owners with logical commands retained or dropped. Previously legal commands
remain valid after rejected new bindings. Recording revalidates all currently
bound prepared sets, not just the last one. The fixture uses the actual issuing
device registry so public resource destruction exercises real unregistering.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-report.yaml`,
`ordinary-focused-results.xml`, `ordinary-regression-results.xml` and
`ordinary-regression-tests.log` in that directory. Contract Complete / Prototype
Complete / scoped Backend Validated only. Public retention versions stay0;
dynamic/push/Graphics/table/indirect/full-domain and RenderDoc/platform/Production
acceptance remain open.


## Ordinary uniform dynamic-offset preparation contract

M4e5b3c2b remains a private Prototype and never changes L2 capability versions.
L2 dynamic-offset values are byte offsets in ascending logical binding order,
with exactly dynamicOffsetBindingCount values. Only direct scalar UniformBuffer
bindings are admitted in this increment. Arrays, storage offsets and missing
native size/alignment facts reject deterministically; no zero substitution or
range inference from buffer length is permitted. Existing no-offset callers
supply count0 and preserve the scalar/array prerequisite behavior.

During actual Compute pipeline creation, retain version1 native buffer facts
from MTLComputePipelineReflection arguments: physical slot, bufferAlignment
(min alignment of starting offset) and bufferDataSize (sizeof declared object).
Unknown/zero facts remain explicit and cannot authorize an offset. These facts
are backend-owned compiled metadata under the existing pipeline provider budget.
The shared function mapper proves the logical-to-physical slot correspondence.
Preparation requires a unique matching fact, positive alignment/span, aligned
input offset and core overflow-safe containment of the full reflected object in
the actual MTLBuffer length. Metadata or device/function mismatch rejects.

The existing immutable descriptor snapshot receives the validated copied byte
offset and the exact queried alignment/span; its dynamic index is consumed by
this deterministic transformation before common function preparation. Recording
and dispatch revalidate these baked fields against the actual pipeline facts
and native buffer length, then the common direct recorder uses the baked byte
offset. Caller offset storage is neither retained nor read during recording.
All newly owned CPU metadata uses the preparation quota; no range-plan vector,
new native resource or per-bind allocation is permitted. Owner counting,
atomic admission, descriptor observations, quota sealing and callback retirement
remain identical to c2a. Non-Apple factory returns native-runtime-unavailable;
Null/Vulkan/WebGPU public budget lowering remains DiagnosticFailure.

Acceptance: actual nonzero-offset A/B data from two opaque uniform blocks;
exact count/order, caller-offset mutation independence, missing/zero/duplicate
facts, alignment, full-byte-span overflow/range and unsupported array/storage
negatives; legal prior state survives rejected preparations. Rebind32/dispatch
allocation0 at an exhausted actual quota; delayed native completion with logical
command retained/dropped, cancellation, fast contracts and Null hidden-frame.
No matrix shader source or matrix storage contract changes are authorized.


M4e5b3c2b evidence: the existing opaque uniform shader reflects actual native
alignment16 with spans96/272, matching its six/seventeen float4 blocks. Both
nonzero A/B offset snapshots produce exact GPU output, including with logical
commands retained/dropped behind an actual native timeline wait. Changing caller
offset storage after preparation has no effect. Rebind32/dispatch remain core
allocation0 at exhausted quota; open/sealed cancellation releases exact
prepared snapshot ownership. Wrong counts, missing inputs, missing/zero/duplicate
facts, late alignment/full-span failures and unsupported storage/array offset
domains reject while preserving legal prior native state. Pure range contracts
cover address-space overflow without adding core allocation. 32 rebuilt
regressions with API Validation pass, plus non-Apple Metal, Vulkan command/device
and WebGPU command syntax. Contract Complete / Prototype Complete / scoped
Backend Validated only. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-offset-report.yaml`,
`ordinary-offset-focused-results.xml`, `ordinary-offset-regression-results.xml`
and `ordinary-offset-regression-tests.log` and `ordinary-offset-native-tests.log`
in that directory. The public
retention versions remain0 and all wider readiness gates remain open.

## Ordinary push preparation prerequisite audit

The audit below is implemented by M4e5b3c2c in the following contract and
evidence section. It grants no public execution capability. Reuse
semantics::validatePushConstantCommand/Binding (AtomicFullBlockPush) and
PushConstantConsumptionState; do not duplicate pipeline/range/stage validators
or infer a byte size from native defaults. The existing generated trace fixture
provides three float4 fields and native readback; the shared push fixture provides
full-block multi-stage contract coverage. Preparation must own an exact copy
under the command's quota, bind it to compiled pipeline/physical slot identity,
preflight its owners with the whole immutable descriptor batch and respect the
unconsumed-update rule. Unknown stage/slot/size or malformed/missing bytes must
fail before native mutation. This audit grants no native push or Graphics
capability and does not close parent M4e5b3c2/M4e5. Four public backend budget
versions remain0 / DiagnosticFailure pending full-domain evidence.


## Ordinary prepared Compute push packet contract

M4e5b3c2c is backend-private Prototype. Preparation produces a construction-key
sealed, noncopyable packet from the concrete issuing ComputePipeline and L2
PushConstantsDesc. The shared AtomicFullBlockPush command/binding validators
must pass first (Compute visibility, offset0, exact sole range, size<=L2 ceiling,
non-null data, exact generated reflection/version/stages). The native pipeline
must be executable on the actual device; its reflected push slot must be valid,
Compute-visible, and identify one actual native buffer fact with requiredBytes
equal to the full L2 block. Unknown/duplicate/zero or conflicting metadata fails
with a stable diagnostic. No stage, size, slot or native payload is guessed.

Packet preparation copies bytes into explicit core PMR storage under the same
CPU quota/budget as ordinary bindings. It captures compiled pipeline/function,
physical slot, byte count and native alignment/span facts. Caller source memory
is not retained. Existing resource/pipeline providers keep their declared
budgets; no new native buffer or shader compilation is introduced. Failure
releases every partial packet allocation; non-Apple preparation rejects with
native-runtime-unavailable, and all four public retention versions remain0.

Ordinary descriptor preparation can observe a push-requiring pipeline without
supplying bytes; dispatch must reject until its exact pipeline has a valid push
packet. The ordinary combined binding call accepts an optional prepared push
packet and projects all descriptor owners plus packet/pipeline owners in one
atomic admission before any native binding/byte/observation mutation. Native
setBytes uses the full immutable copy at the validated physical slot. Raw push
recording remains gated. A push update while PushConstantConsumptionState is
pending rejects PC-E013; descriptor-only rebind keeps the prior valid push.
Switching pipeline while pending also rejects; after consumption a switch resets
push identity so old bytes cannot authorize a new pipeline. Only a successful
native direct dispatch consumes pending state. Pass end closes that lifecycle
without pretending to consume; abandonment/poison clear aliases. Completion
retention owns packets until actual callback retirement even with commands
retained or destroyed. A rejected packet/binding preserves the previous legal
native state; missing/foreign/mutated quota, slot or facts fail closed.

Acceptance reuses the existing generated DDGI trace shader (three float4 fields,
no matrix changes), existing shared push validators and range fixtures. Test
exact ray A/B numeric output from different immutable bytes, caller mutation
independence, required-push dispatch gate, full atomic owner rejection,
malformed size/offset/stages/source/layout/reflection and native slot/fact/device
negatives, pending update/pipeline switch, preparation quota exhaustion,
rebind/dispatch allocation0, open/sealed cancellation, actual delayed completion,
fast contract and Null hidden-frame. Numeric evidence grants no RenderDoc,
ordinary Graphics, platform or Production Ready claim.


M4e5b3c2c evidence: 32 rebuilt regressions pass with Metal API Validation,
including quick contracts, shader/matrix tests and Null hidden-frame. Existing
DDGI trace/push artifacts supply the exact 48-byte block and actual native
slot/alignment/span. One dynamic triangle produces exact A=5/B=7 distance,
material3, normalY1 and emissive.5 with untouched output padding. Caller bytes
are mutated after preparation without changing GPU results. Whole-owner
admission counts10 first / 15 A+B and rejects capacity0/1/one-less before any
native mutation. Rebind32/dispatch allocate0 at exhausted core quota. Required
push, malformed block/reflection, actual slot/facts, pending update/switch,
failed-dispatch nonconsumption, foreign quota/pipeline and new-pipeline byte
invalidation pass. Partial CPU preparation cleanup, hostile PMR default,
open/sealed cancellation, wait metadata failure and timeline-delayed native
completion with logical command retained/dropped pass. Non-Apple Metal,
Vulkan command/device and WebGPU command syntax checks pass.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-push-report.yaml`,
`ordinary-push-focused-results.xml`, `ordinary-push-regression-results.xml`,
`ordinary-push-regression-tests.log` and `ordinary-push-native-tests.log` in that
directory. Contract Complete / Prototype Complete / scoped Backend Validated
only. Public retention and Metal Graphics versions stay0. Parent c2/c/M4e5,
Graphics/table/indirect, nonempty queries, RenderDoc, wider platform and
Production Ready acceptance remain incomplete.


## Ordinary prepared Compute storage-image evidence contract

M4e5b3c2d extends private Compute evidence using the existing generic function
preparation/recording and issuing registry, not an additional execution path.
Only the tested Texture2D Rgba16Float StorageTexture domain is claimed here.
L2 layout and view must explicitly agree on storage format; Unknown rejects.
Generated reflection supplies resource type/stage/array and physical bindings,
not a storage-format inference. Existing dimension/usage/liveness validators
remain mandatory. The reusable validateStorageTextureViewFormat helper checks
exact authored correspondence before native argument creation and again before
binding/dispatch against registry and native payload metadata. This helper does
not grant native format support. Public ordinary legacy descriptor paths are
not certified by it; all four public budget adapters remain closed.
Preparation observes the logical
view/parent lifetime and exact registered native view/allocation ownership;
retaining a native texture does not permit consumption after its logical view
or parent is destroyed. Every binding/dispatch reuses existing validators.
The exact copied descriptor snapshot and native view survive legal in-flight
work until real GPU completion/cancellation under the same core quota.

Acceptance uses existing ddgi_atlas_fill Slang artifacts/reflection with its
8x8 threads and queried dimensions/bounds. Two explicit 3x2 storage textures
receive exact .25/.5/.75/1 and .75/.25/.5/1 float4 values; texture-to-buffer
copy must preserve aligned row padding. Whole binding owner admission precedes
native mutation; previous legal A survives rejected B. Duplicate rebind32 and
dispatch allocate0 at exhausted quota. View/parent/revision, physical mapping,
format and foreign resource failures close before consumption. Delayed native
completion is tested with logical commands retained/dropped, together with
open/sealed cancellation, quick contracts and Null hidden-frame.
Null/Vulkan/WebGPU and public Metal CommandRetentionBudget lowering remain
DiagnosticFailure/version0; their existing resource semantics are unchanged.
No Graphics, RenderDoc visual, wider platform or Production Ready claim follows
from numeric Compute texture readback.


M4e5b3c2d evidence: 32 rebuilt regressions pass with Metal API Validation.
Actual 3x2 RGBA16F storage writes match both explicit full float4 values and

13 with the two readback buffers; capacity0/1/one-less rejects before native
binding. Rejected B leaves A executable. Missing/wrong layout/view format,

## Next ordinary Graphics preparation prerequisite audit

M4e5b3c2e remains Contract in progress. The selected entry names can be borrowed

## SingleEntryShaderArtifact/1 prerequisite

Publication update (2026-10-05): ordinary codegen now emits publication3 with
binary-verified buffer declaration metadata; publication4 preserves the explicit
WGSL-unavailable /2 file-role contract. Legacy1/2 remains whole-buffer-only and
cannot supply declaration queries. See original-buffer-declaration-custody-contract.md
for the strict schema, binary/reflection/owner checks and validation evidence.
Target minimums and native slices remain unproved; the /1 prerequisite below
continues to describe the legacy file-role and immutable owner boundary.

M4e5b3c2e1 defines an ordinary shader-domain immutable startup artifact; it is
not a pipeline or execution ticket. A YAML publication explicitly declares
`type: SingleEntryShaderArtifact`, `contractVersion: 1`, `matrixLayout: row-major`,
`entry`, `stage` (Vertex/Fragment/Compute), and exactly five `files` roles:
source, spirv, metal, wgsl, reflection. Every role declares a flat filename and
SHA-256 digest. Paths/roles may not alias. All five nonempty files are read once
under explicit per-file/total byte ceilings and retained as immutable core-owned
bytes. Reflection declares matching recorded target filenames, source digest,
exactly one entry/name/stage and valid ShaderReflection/3. Unknown types, missing
counts/visibility or ambiguous/duplicate logical bindings reject; no parser
fallback supplies executable semantics. Generated push member validation is
reused. An explicit binding-count ceiling bounds reflection work.

The same explicit CPU quota charges retained bytes, parsed reflection, helpers
and the artifact/control block; partial failures retire all core allocations.
Core PMR storage is explicit even under a hostile process PMR default. Existing
fkYAML parsing internals and filesystem/stream implementations remain outside
the core allocation accounting, bounded by authored input file byte ceilings;
this limitation forbids a full memory/performance or Production Ready claim.
Loading/parsing/filesystem access is startup only. No later file edits or caller
publication lifetime may change retained bytes/metadata.

Ordinary codegen must compile one selected entry through the existing Slang
cross-target/reflection pipeline before emitting this publication. It must not
split an already combined native map. Every target keeps explicit row-major
compilation. Missing target or unavailable WGSL rejects publication. The existing
bindless package retains its own authorization/lowering semantics; generic
ordinary artifact loading must reject bindless reflection and cannot grant a
BindlessCross capability. Four public bounded recording adapters remain
DiagnosticFailure/version0; this prerequisite alone opens no Graphics gate.
Fast artifact identity/budget/immutability negatives and Null hidden-frame are
required. Backend/platform/RenderDoc/Production readiness is not established
by successful artifact loading.

M4e5b3c2e1 evidence: four real Slang single-entry publications pass immutable
identity/input/binding/quota/partial-cleanup and hostile PMR negative contracts.
The production shadow_depth matrix array exposed a reflection CLI transparent
wrapper and missing numeric MatrixStride. Codegen now follows only an explicit
single full-range offset0 child, obtains stride from the binary member's
ColMajor/MatrixStride contract and rejects incompatible/nontransparent data.
Generated float[48]/224B size and member offsets compile. Five codegen guards
and 35 rebuilt regressions, including matrix probes and Null hidden-frame,
pass. Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for this artifact prerequisite;
no new Backend/Platform Validated or Production Ready claim. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/single-entry-report.yaml`,
`single-entry-regression-results.xml` and `single-entry-regression-tests.log`.

## Ordinary per-entry lowering prerequisite

M4e5b3c2e2a converts a sealed SingleEntryShaderArtifact/1 to an ordinary L2
single-entry interface and lowers the exact selected Vertex/Fragment through
the same MetalBindingSpace/1 validator used by bindless. The interface copies
only exact reflected logical binding/type/count/stage/depth/input-attachment
and push-member facts, with source/target names from the sealed publication.
It grants no authorization. Entry/name/stage must match the sealed artifact;
merged reflection, missing physical metadata, duplicate active/inactive maps,
unsupported framebuffer-fetch and nonordinary shader ABI reject. Explicit
inactive physical records are retained; they are never inferred from L2
visibility or native liveness. Lowering clears output before validation and
publishes only a complete result. All new reflection/map/interface container
storage uses explicit core PMR, including a hostile process PMR default.
Startup lowering is charged under the caller's explicit core quota; exhausted
quota rejects with a stable diagnostic and partial allocations retire.

Null/Vulkan/WebGPU ordinary interface facts are backend-neutral; their bounded
execution gates remain DiagnosticFailure/version0. Metal's new lowering is a
private preparation prerequisite, with no native pipeline/recording/RenderDoc
claim. Actual selected artifact retention during native pipeline construction,
provider ownership/budget and preparation are e2b and remain required before
e2 can be checked. Fast sealed fixture mapping/identity/negative/quota guards
and Null hidden-frame are required for e2a.

M4e5b3c2e2a evidence: 36 rebuilt regressions pass, including sealed ordinary
VS/FS/Compute/shadow conversion and mapping, explicit inactive records,
entry/stage/duplicate/status/out-of-range negatives, hostile PMR positive and
negative diagnostics, exhausted/1-byte quota with full cleanup, matrix probes
and Null hidden-frame. L2 reflection/interface defaults and ValidationResult
construction/copy now select explicit core resources. Contract Complete /
Prototype Complete for per-entry lowering only. No native execution or public
version changes. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-stage-report.yaml`,
`ordinary-stage-regression-results.xml`, `ordinary-stage-regression-tests.log`.

## Ordinary Graphics native provider ownership

M4e5b3c2e2b1 is an explicitly opted-in private startup provider. It accepts
one sealed Vertex artifact and an optional sealed Fragment artifact, an
explicit version1 CPU/provider budget and its exact shared quota. Every
artifact must already be charged to that same quota; the provider cannot
retain uncharged external bytes. Both entries must declare the same source
SHA-256/name identity. The moved authored RenderPipelineDesc supplies a live
L2 layout and complete state but no shader package/entries; unsupported
subpass/table/tessellation/indirect/framebuffer-fetch ABI reject. Selected
entry names/functions/stages come solely from the sealed artifacts.

Logical descriptors are merged only when type/count/depth/input-attachment
semantics match; visibility is the exact union. Physical stage maps remain
separate. Nonempty push blocks must agree in complete members/size/slot and
version; stage masks are explicitly unioned. Lowering uses the shared per-entry
validator and derives vertex stream reservations from Vertex only. Omitted
Fragment must pass the existing exact depth-only stage contract. All optional
features must pass the explicit Metal native capability lowering contract.

Creation uses sealed copied MSL bytes and the actual native compilation path;
no filesystem reloading or failed-payload placeholder is accepted. Provider
owns both artifacts, their stage layouts, merged immutable logical interface,
consumption contract and real native payload under the same core quota. Moving
caller state/retiring its L2 layout does not change retained state. Core-owned
state allocator identity is required; default STL/PMR ownership is not adopted.
Failure releases all provider allocations while caller artifacts keep their
existing ownership. This is preparation-only and grants no bounded Graphics
recording authorization, no bindless authorization and no public version.

Public Null/Vulkan/WebGPU bounded execution remains DiagnosticFailure. Private
Metal stub creation returns NATIVE-RUNTIME-UNAVAILABLE; public
CommandRetentionBudget and Metal Graphics versions remain0. Actual
native function/pipeline existence proves only this startup path, never draw,
output, completion, platform or RenderDoc readiness. Subsequent descriptor
preparation and complete owner recording remain e2b2 and later phases.
Required verification: same-quota/source/stage/omission/negative ownership,
real Metal pipeline payloads with caller sources dropped, complete partial
cleanup, quick contracts and Null hidden-frame.

M4e5b3c2e2b1 evidence: 37 regressions pass with Metal API Validation, including
23 rebuilt executable targets across focused/full runs, fast contracts and
Null hidden-frame. Real separate VS/FS libraries/functions/render pipeline
and exact depth-only omitted Fragment are created from copied sealed bytes
with source files removed. Caller state/layout/artifact owners can be dropped;
provider retains exact source/maps/native payload and consumption under its
same quota. Wrong source/stage/quota/backend/allocator/opt-in/layout/feature,
invalid omission and exhausted quota reject without provider storage leaks.
Actual invalid MSL compilation rejects with native failure diagnostic and

PMR construction from the native error-message conditional. Strict active
physical type/name/key/slot/count facts precede the legacy cache parser;
100 artifact mutation cases pass. Metal stub/private lowering and
Vulkan/WebGPU command/device contract source syntax pass using the Apple SDK;
this is not a non-Apple target build. Contract Complete / Prototype Complete /
Backend Validated solely for private startup pipeline creation. No draw/output,
completion/cancellation, RenderDoc, new Platform Validated or Production Ready

Evidence:

Next e2b2 audit: reuse MetalRhiNativeFunctionBindingPreparation and its exact
function name/type/device checks, descriptor-copy preflight and accumulated
argument encoder/count/encoded/allocated byte reservations. The current shared
Graphics helper has an unconditional nonzero argument-budget guard; a proved
direct-only ordinary path must admit0 without loosening public bindless
budget/authorization validation. DescriptorConsumptionContract owns logical
bindings/set identities but not all authored layout flags (dynamic offsets,

## Ordinary Graphics descriptor preparation

M4e5b3c2e2b2 retains an immutable exact descriptor-set layout contract in the
provider: explicit set/name and all authored binding fields, copied with core
PMR storage. Preparation validates allocation, issuing registry, complete
written resource identities and exact authored shape through shared validators;
missing/foreign/stale/mismatched metadata fails before native encoding. Dynamic
offsets remain DiagnosticFailure until Graphics buffer facts/offset lowering
are separately specified. No guessed offsets or binding formats are allowed.

Prepared storage must own the provider, copied descriptor group and exact
compiled per-entry bindings under that provider's same explicit CPU quota.
Budget version1/opt-in, CPU quota and argument count/encoded/allocated byte
ceilings are checked. The shared function preflight validates actual function
name/type/device, resource shape/device and per-entry physical footprint for
both stages before allocation of argument buffers. Matching count/byte budgets0
are valid only for an actual direct/inactive-only function; active arrays must
reject at count admission. Public BindlessBindingBudget validation and public
version0 execution gates are unchanged. The copied per-entry layout/functions
must remain exact when observations are revalidated.

Common ordinary descriptor observation owns its lifetime ticket, content
revision and issuing registry; Compute and Graphics consume the same validator
for liveness, revision, resource/view/allocation identity and explicit storage
format. Preparation failures retire all new CPU/native argument storage.
Prepared Graphics projects every provider/source/map/interface/layout/native
function/pipeline, descriptor/resource/view/allocation and argument-buffer
owner as startup core-owned storage; duplicate owners may be projected but
recorder admission must use the existing distinct-owner algorithm atomically.
Dropping caller provider/layout/source owners cannot change prepared storage;
mutating or destroying logical descriptors/resources rejects observation.

No draw/record/present/completion path opens from preparation. Null/Vulkan/
WebGPU public bounded execution remains DiagnosticFailure/version0; Metal stub
preparation fails NATIVE-RUNTIME-UNAVAILABLE. Contract and Null smoke, actual
Metal scalar/array/depth-only preparation, function/metadata mutations, budgets
and retirement are required. Backend evidence proves preparation only; native
output/RenderDoc/platform/Production readiness remains later work.

## Bounded ordinary Graphics pass preparation and recording

M4e5b3c3 first separates render-pass creation/attachment lowering from encoder
recording. M4e5b3c3a is preparation only: reuse one native attachment lowering
for legacy begin and sealed ordinary preparation, with exact issuing registry,
view/parent lifetime, device, format, extent, sample, load/store/resolve and
layer contracts. Metal's specified eight color slots bound the fixed native
attachment observation array to eight color + eight resolve + one depth view.
No authored attachment can overflow that limit or be silently truncated.
Shared closed-enum operation validation rejects unknown load/store values with
RHI-RENDER-PASS-ATTACHMENT-OP-INVALID before mutation in all four public backends
and Metal native preparation; valid L2 operations keep their existing lowering.
The prepared pass owns its native descriptor, exact views/native objects and
allocation tokens, provider and all metadata under the same explicit quota;
no caller RenderPassDesc or label string is adopted. Preparation rejects
foreign/missing/stale/sealed resources and incompatible provider state.
Recording must revalidate every observation; a vanished logical view or parent,
changed ownership/device/format or write seal rejects before native mutation.

The first later recorder domain is direct triangle draws without vertex/index,
push, secondary, indirect, subpass or query payloads. Those paths remain
DiagnosticFailure. Its full prepared pass/provider/binding owner batch must be
atomically admitted before creating an encoder or binding a pipeline, and
native encoder ownership storage must be preallocated during command startup.
BoundDescriptorSlots supplies completeness/revision checks, repeated binding
uses the shared exact function validator, and pipeline/pass transitions reset
tracked sets. Completion/cancellation consume the existing sealed owner store.
No CPU container growth, argument creation, shader compilation, file IO or
blocking waits are permitted in successful binding/draw/pass end.

Four-backend conclusion for c3a: Metal Native preparation only on Apple and
DiagnosticFailure in stubs; Null remains Validator/version0; Vulkan/WebGPU
bounded ordinary Graphics recording remains DiagnosticFailure/version0.
Preparation does not open any public/private draw gate. c3b must prove atomic
admission, zero-allocation binding/data, native completion and abandonment;
RenderDoc visual evidence and platform readiness remain separate gates.

c3b requires a sealed pass plus initial prepared binding on begin. Pass and
binding must share the same provider, registry and command quota; whole pass,
whole binding and the command's preallocated encoder slot are admitted as one
indexed projection. A native encoder creation/adoption failure after admission
poisons the command and releases it through terminal abandonment. No retry
can replay that partially admitted command. Rebinding compatible providers
resets descriptor state only after successful complete preflight/admission;
failed preflight preserves prior owners/state/native pipeline. A changed
provider cannot inherit previously bound sets. The first subset requires
dynamic viewport/scissor, TriangleList, sampleCount1, no push constants and
no reflected vertex streams. All other paths keep deterministic diagnostics.
Pass begin explicitly writes a full attachment-sized viewport/scissor; native
defaults are not consumed. End and every draw revalidate pass observations;
draw also revalidates each selected prepared set and logical completeness.
Public versions remain0, and stubs reject the new private entry points.
The first recording allowlist is non-depth sampled textures with plain samplers;
buffer/storage/input-attachment/comparison/depth sampling remains closed. Actual
MTLRenderPipelineReflection (ArgumentInfo + BufferTypeInfo) must record each
stage's direct or argument-buffer texture type, scalar data type and depth flag.
Nested array elements use the queried argumentIndex/argumentIndexStride/length,
never semantic binding numbers. Each consumed texture must resolve exactly one
fact, match the actual native texture type and use the float texel ABI supported
by existing L2 texture formats. Missing/duplicate/unqueried facts or mismatches
fail before owner admission; source names and backend/OS guesses are forbidden.

c3b is Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for the private sampled subset,
with scoped Backend Validated Metal data and lifetime evidence. Exact scalar
and three-texture array 4x4 bytes, 34/39 distinct owners, zero core allocations
at exhausted metadata quota, ABI/resource negatives and real completion pass.
Evidence: `build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-recording-report.yaml`.
RenderDoc visual acceptance, Platform Validated and Production Ready remain open.

c3c composes already approved private render and texture-transfer operations in
one command. The same resource-owner storage must atomically admit the union;
no category may infer an additional budget from another category's capacity.
A copy refused before native mutation preserves the completed render work and
prior owners and can be terminally abandoned or sealed without the copy.
Repeated prepared render passes reuse the preallocated render encoder slot.
Native encoder boundaries provide the existing Metal resource ordering; a
transfer may not occur inside a live render pass. Submission moves the entire
sealed union once to the existing native-callback owner; retained/dropped
logical commands cannot change retirement. Existing blit encoder construction
is still quota-charged allocation and has no allocation-free begin claim.
Null is Validator/version0; Vulkan/WebGPU bounded composition and unavailable
Metal stubs are DiagnosticFailure/version0. This composition prerequisite does
not open any public retention/indexed Graphics or visual/platform gate.

c3c is Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for the same private subset and
scoped Backend Validated with Metal API Validation. Exact 36/41-owner unions,
one-less copy refusal without partial retention, render/copy32/render reuse,
zero core allocations after blit begin under exhausted quota, encoder-overlap
rejection and exact GPU data pass. Unique transfer destinations and prepared
bindings survive a native timeline wait then retire together at completion;
open/sealed cancellation and retained/dropped commands pass. Quick core and
Null hidden-frame verification accompany the native test. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-composition-report.yaml`.

The next ordinary Graphics prerequisite (c4) is per-selected-stage buffer
reflection. Compute facts cannot stand in for Vertex/Fragment facts, and an
argument-buffer's reflected storage span cannot stand in for its members'
buffer spans. Before enabling uniform ranges, preparation must own exact native
slot/alignment/span facts under its quota, resolve them against the sealed
entry map and copied L2 range, and reject missing/ambiguous/foreign facts.
AtomicFullBlockPush must be extended with independent stage consumption before
push opens; vertex/index/storage remain closed until their own contracts.
These gates do not change the c3 sampled subset or any public capability.

c4a captures buffer facts in two independent stage stores, each with explicit
stage/version and the existing Compute buffer-fact value. Storage is reserved
before native compilation under provider quota, using the specified Metal
buffer-slot domain already defined by MetalVertexStreamMapping. The shared
signature collector appends only within reserved capacity for Graphics;
unknown slots/kinds/overflow fail pipeline creation with a deterministic
diagnostic. A selected queried stage has version1 even when its buffer list is
empty; absent Fragment remains None/version0. Native alignment/span zero stays
unknown and is rejected by the existing uniform range validator. No Compute
fact or argument-buffer fact is substituted for another stage/member. Metal
is Native fact preparation, stubs DiagnosticFailure; Null Validator and
Vulkan/WebGPU bounded Graphics execution retain version0/DiagnosticFailure.
This is buffer reflection evidence only, not uniform/push execution acceptance.

c4a is Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for preparation and scoped
Backend Validated for actual Metal reflection. Fragment argument-buffer slot0
has queried alignment8/span24; Vertex is queried-empty, scalar stages are
queried-empty and absent Fragment is None/version0 with zero storage. Stores
are core quota-owned; hostile PMR, source removal, exhausted preparation and

regressions (including core/contract and Null smoke) and eleven Apple SDK syntax
checks pass; these are not MinGW/WebGPU target builds. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-buffer-facts-report.yaml`.
The next c4b gate is copied direct uniform ranges with independent stage facts
and native nonzero-offset data; it remains unimplemented/DiagnosticFailure.

c4b expands only the private ordinary Graphics direct UniformBuffer domain.
L2 descriptor ownership/range intent is unchanged: ordered dynamic offsets
are copied at preparation, with zero used only for an explicitly non-dynamic
binding. Each active selected stage resolves its own direct physical slot and
requires its own queried nonzero alignment/span. The shared Compute uniform
range validator checks actual native buffer length for every stage; different
stage spans are retained independently, never merged or guessed. The prepared
descriptor captures stage/slot/alignment/span/offset tuples under its quota.
It also freezes the native buffer/device/allocation-token identities projected
into owner storage; replacing a native object inside the same logical payload
must reject before native access. The reusable native resource observation
compares both object pointers and ownership identities without allocation.
Record and draw recheck the tuples against live native facts before owner or
encoder mutation. Missing/wrong-stage/duplicate/zero facts, array-member uniform
buffers, unsupported dynamic domains, out-of-range/misaligned offsets and
changed captured offsets fail with stable diagnostics. The complete descriptor,
buffer/native allocation and provider projection uses existing atomic admission
and completion retirement; no new dynamic storage appears on recording.
Buffer arrays/storage/push/vertex/index remain closed. Metal is Native private
lowering only after real nonzero-offset data and lifetime tests pass; stubs
DiagnosticFailure. Null remains Validator, Vulkan/WebGPU bounded ordinary
execution stays DiagnosticFailure; all public retention/Graphics versions stay0.
RenderDoc and platform/Production acceptance are separate prerequisites.
The Vertex-only depth case requires the prepared pass sample count to be copied
from its validated depth resource, exactly as for a color attachment; zero is
not a depth-only substitute. Null/Vulkan/WebGPU retain their existing attachment
sample-count validation; this corrects shared Metal preparation metadata without
altering L2 attachment or multisample semantics.

c4b is Contract Complete / Prototype Complete for private direct uniform
preparation/recording and scoped Backend Validated on actual Metal. Shared VS/FS
alignment16/span32 and fragment-only alignment16/span16 are queried separately.
Static red and nonzero A/B exact 4x4 RGBA, plus Vertex-only D32 zero, pass with
retained/dropped commands. Native object/token mutation, ordered copied offsets,
late facts/captures, stale resource, budget, cancellation and delayed completion
negatives pass. Distinct owner counts are 35 color, 26 depth and 42 A/B union;
one-less rebind refuses atomically and preserves native A output. Accepted
begin/bind32/draw/end allocate zero core objects at exhausted quota/hostile PMR.
Owning legacy ValidationResult diagnostics on rejected observations may allocate
bounded temporary strings; this is not an allocation-free rejection or full
CommandRetentionBudget claim. Core native identity observations preserve object
release before the final allocation token, with CPU-only identity/order tests
and separate actual native mutation/retirement evidence.

Twenty-seven executable targets rebuilt; forty-one regressions including fast
contracts, 158 real publication cases, Null hidden-frame and actual Metal pass.
Twelve Apple SDK syntax checks and two actual SPIR-V validations pass; none is
a target MinGW/WebGPU/platform build. Evidence:
`build/mac-metal-debug/artifacts/bindless-prepared-graphics/ordinary-graphics-uniform-report.yaml`.
RenderDoc remains unavailable, all public versions stay0 and uniform arrays,
storage, push and vertex/index/indirect/query remain gated. The next c4c step is
owned complete Graphics push blocks with per-stage consumption; this remains
unimplemented/DiagnosticFailure.

c4c contract: AtomicFullBlockPush/1 keeps its shared command and exact range /
generated reflection checks. The provider owns the authored push-range projection
after the source layout dies. Preparation copies the entire block into the same
quota-owned storage used by ordinary Compute push. The requested visibility must
equal the generated active push stages, not a partial stage update. Every active
selected entry independently resolves its sealed push slot and actual native
buffer alignment/span; the queried span must equal the complete reflected block.
Unknown, duplicate, wrong-stage or mutated facts reject before encoder mutation.
The frozen provider, selected functions and native pipeline ownership identities
must still match. No source-only slot or dummy buffer authorizes execution.

Begin/bind atomically admits the full pass/binding/push/provider owner projection
before native changes. A pending unconsumed update rejects another push or a
provider transition. A successful draw consumes the update; subsequent draws may
reuse it. Changing providers clears the push, so a fresh complete block is needed
even when the new provider has an identical ABI. Pass end/cancellation reset the
encoder-local state without counting as draw consumption. Successful recording
uses no owned-byte allocation; Metal setVertexBytes/setFragmentBytes are the
actual private execution payload. Retained owners retire only at real completion
or unsubmitted cancellation. Owning diagnostic strings retain their separately
documented rejection allocation limitation.

Four-backend decision: Metal Native only inside this explicit private prototype
after native data/lifetime tests; unavailable Metal runtime DiagnosticFailure.
Null remains Validator; Vulkan/WebGPU bounded ordinary Graphics push stays
DiagnosticFailure. Public retention and Metal Graphics versions stay0. RenderDoc,
target platform, full command domain and Production Ready gates remain open.
Status: Contract Complete / Prototype Complete, scoped Backend Validated for
actual private Metal payloads. VS/FS each query slot0/alignment16/span32. Exact
4x4 A/B RGBA8 [64,128,192,255] / [192,64,128,255] and Vertex-only D32 0.25 / 0.5
pass. Source byte overwrite, source layout destruction, exact provider identity,
unknown/duplicate/changed span/alignment/stage facts, foreign/partial blocks,
unconsumed updates, identical-ABI provider freshness and native object mutation
are guarded. Begin admits 30 color or 24 depth owners; two-provider union49.
One-less admission preserves native A and produces its exact data. Successful
begin/bind/draw/end uses allocation0 under exhausted quota/hostile PMR. Open/sealed
cancellation and real wait-delayed completion retire push and provider owners,
with commands retained/dropped. No visual/target-platform acceptance follows.

The shared provider merge emits canonical VS|FS reflection rather than the
unsupported Vertex|Fragment spelling. Shader publication also fails with
SHADER-PUSH-SOURCE-DECLARATION-UNSUPPORTED when an authored push declaration
cannot resolve to a supported nonempty source block; it cannot silently publish
zero-byte reflection. The fixture uses the existing direct struct declaration
contract and pure float4 fields, with SPIR-V/MSL/WGSL and YAML member reflection.
Twenty-eight rebuilt executable targets and forty-two regressions pass, including
194 real artifact cases, fast contracts and Null hidden-frame. Thirteen Apple
SDK syntax checks and two actual SPIR-V validations pass. Evidence:

c4d contract: shared prepared-function snapshots freeze each canonical descriptor
element's native device/object/allocation ownership before argument encoding.
Direct and array members use the same observation, without choosing a resource
from competing payload shapes. The shared preflight/record validators compare
both pointer and shared ownership identity before any native object access.
Generated argument storage separately freezes its actual buffer/device identity;
changing a mutable prepared buffer member cannot substitute a new allocation
after owner admission. Ordinary observation compares these same frozen identities
to registered resources at bind and draw/dispatch. Replacement must fail with
RHI-METAL-PREPARED-RESOURCE-NATIVE-IDENTITY-MISMATCH or
RHI-METAL-PREPARED-ARGUMENT-STORAGE-IDENTITY-MISMATCH before GPU mutation.
Unprepared legacy descriptor paths do not gain certification from these checks.
Observations are quota-owned, fixed fields and reuse NativeResourceObservation;
recording adds no metadata allocation. Original identities survive cancellation
and real completion even if the logical payload is edited later.

Validated for actual private Metal identity/data/lifetime. Scalar/array/sampler
Graphics begin/bind/draw replacements and same-pointer ownership aliases reject
before native access, preserving accepted owner counts and argument reuse stats.
Generated Graphics/Compute argument storage replacements reject independently.
Direct/array Compute binding/dispatch checks reject, while accepted bind32 and
dispatch remain allocation0 at exhausted quota/hostile PMR. Four real delayed
completion tests (direct/array, retained/dropped commands) replace input objects
after encoding; frozen originals remain alive until native completion and then
retire, with exact GPU output. Existing cancellation/lifecycle and 4x4 Graphics
data remain passing. CPU-only canonical/ownership probes grant no native claim.

c4e contract: one reusable backend ownership visitor enumerates the prepared
function, native pipeline/device/libraries/functions/state, descriptor snapshot,
issuing registry, canonical resource payload/device/object/allocation tokens,
and generated argument storage. Compute and Graphics must consume this same
enumeration; transitive native owners cannot be hidden behind one logical payload.
The outer wrapper is separately admitted. Duplicate identities use the existing
bounded retainer's documented deduplication. Preparation freezes the full ordered
projection under its CPU quota; bind/draw/dispatch revalidates pointer AND shared
ownership identity using the visitor without materializing a temporary vector.
Pipeline/descriptor native-member replacement rejects with a stable diagnostic
before native access or owner/encoder mutation. Exact native owner budgets change
where prior Compute enumeration counted only logical payloads.

One-less initial and transition budgets must refuse the entire new batch while

c4f contract: private bounded transfer admission projects the exact canonical
registered payload, native device/object/allocation and placement-heap owners,
plus the issuing registry. Staging uses its existing typed native inspection
(device/buffer and opaque page payload), never a guessed resource shape. A
fixed backend projection uses the same canonical resource utility as descriptor
functions; all nonnull identities are frozen into prepared bounded storage
before any copy. Whole-batch refusal cannot retain a partial native graph.
Post-encode mutation of logical payload fields cannot replace admitted owners
or release original GPU storage/accounting before completion/cancellation.
Token/heap owners precede native objects and wrappers at admission; core bounded
retention releases in reverse admission order to keep backing ownership alive
through final object release. This ordering is explicit and tested rather than
relying on STL destruction order. Repeated copies remain allocation0 after
encoder preparation, with no metadata budget inferred from GPU byte sizes.

c4g contract: direct ordinary Graphics read-only storage-buffer intent uses
L2 ReadOnlyStorageBuffer, a single Buffer descriptor element, exact registered
full resource bytes and no dynamic storage offset. Each selected VS/FS entry
must have authoritative compiler resource/access metadata and queried native
slot/alignment/nonzero minimum type span. Preparation captures and recording
revalidates these independent stage facts and the canonical frozen native
identity. Native buffer size must contain the queried span; zero/unknown facts
fail closed. This proves the native ABI type footprint, not arbitrary shader
index safety; algorithms must respect the authored full-resource extent.
Writable storage, arrays/argument members, storage offsets, vertex/index and
other resource domains remain independently gated. Source/artifact work occurs
only during cold preparation. Complete owner graphs and whole-batch admission
reuse the same projection as Uniform and sampled resources.

c4h audit: the current ordinary provider retains native stream slot mapping but
not the authoritative authored stride/rate/attribute byte layout after pipeline
creation. Bounded vertex recording must first own the L2 layout and finite fetch
footprint, relate it to the selected-entry shader interface and native lowering,
and validate per-Vertex/per-Instance draw extents using existing unsigned range
utilities. Preparation freezes exact source native/device/object/allocation/
registry owners; full batch admission precedes any vertex slot mutation.
Nonindexed draw ranges must use declared first/count/stride/rate/attributes,
without reading back or guessing index contents. Indexed extents remain closed
until a separate deterministic range contract is proven. Existing instancing
fixture, VertexStreamBinding lowering and core allocator/range helpers are the
reuse entrances. Null is Validator; Metal stays private Prototype pending
actual evidence; absent runtime, Vulkan/WebGPU bounded ordinary domain remain
DiagnosticFailure. Public versions0, quick contracts/Null/native data/lifetime
and separate RenderDoc/platform gates remain mandatory.
Status: audited prerequisite; L2 ownership/range implementation is next.

c4h1 contract: VertexInputConsumption/1 owns a flat, exact copy of authored
stream slot/stride/input rate and attribute location/offset/format. Supported
formats are the four existing L2 Float32x1..4 types; their byte footprints come
from the explicit 32-bit scalar format definition. Unknown formats/rates,
empty or zero-stride declared streams and duplicate slots/locations diagnose
before native creation. Empty overall vertex input is valid. No stride or
attribute location is inferred from shader source or native defaults. The
selected-entry artifact must match every authored attribute location/type.
Attribute offsets may overlap successive strides as the existing L2 descriptor
permits; the maximum declared attribute end defines the true fetch footprint.

For a nonindexed draw, each Vertex-rate stream uses firstVertex/vertexCount;
each Instance-rate stream uses firstInstance/instanceCount. Buffer binding
byte offset and actual byte extent are explicit. Validation uses a finite
number of complete fetch footprints and core unsigned half-open range checks;
it never multiplies an unvalidated first/count into an overflowing address.
Successful range/match checks allocate nothing. Layout capture uses core PMR
under the actual preparation quota. The ordinary provider retains this L2
contract plus the approved native slot mapping before authored state dies.
The existing vertex execution gate stays closed until c4h2 proves its prepared
owner admission and actual data/completion/cancellation. Indexed/indirect
extents remain an independent contract, never guessed from vertex counts.

C4h2 contract (implementation pending): a private prepared vertex binding

must contain at least one complete declared fetch footprint. Runtime observes

Provider switches and pass boundaries clear all stream observations; native
